### 2026-09-02 05:00:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Oil Inventories Resume Drawdown — US crude oil inventories fell by 2.6 million barrels in the week ending August 28th, according to the American Petroleum Institute (API), following a 4.2 million-barrel increase in the previous week. Commercial crude inventories excluding the SPR have declined by just over 48 million barrels over the past 20 weeks, while total US crude inventories are up 3.1 million barrels year-to-date. Another 3.1 million barrels left the SPR, bringing total reserves to 286.6 million barrels, within the generally accepted operational minimum of 250-300 million barrels. US crude production rose to 13.843 million bpd in the week ending August 21st from 13.830 million bpd, up 461,000 bpd year-on-year. Meanwhile, gasoline inventories increased by 300,000 barrels after falling 3.2 million barrels the previous week, while distillate stocks declined by 300,000 barrels following a 500,000-barrel draw. Cushing inventories rose by 200,000 barrels after increasing by 1 million barrels previously.

The multi-year covert program, code-named C430L, assembled some of Russia’s most experienced missile experts to help Iran build a new class of weapon capable of threatening US aircraft carriers and other regional warships. Investigators say the project began in 2023 and continued after the US and Israel launched a war on Iran. Cited evidence includes leaked Russian communications, travel records and analysis of military patents. The program’s primary aim is to help Iran develop a ramjet/scramjet air‑breathing propulsion system capable of sustained flight at multiple times the speed of sound; weapons experts say the technology is almost certainly intended for anti-ship and land-attack cruise missiles.

Ibovespa Extends Winning Streak to Ten Sessions — The Ibovespa rose 1.3% to close at 179,722 on Tuesday, extending its winning streak to ten sessions amid renewed expectations for continued Selic easing. Brazil’s GDP grew more than expected in 2Q26, driven mainly by agriculture, while the detailed breakdown was weaker, signaling that economic momentum is fading despite a tight labor market and short-term demand stimulus measures. For the BCB, the data suggest that interest rates have already done much of the work, leaving room for further Selic cuts. Major banks advanced, with Banco do Brasil up 2.3%, Bradesco 1.2% and Itaú 0.9%. B3 gained 2.5% despite lower broker estimates, as its buy recommendation was maintained. Petrobras jumped 4.1%, tracking higher oil prices and benefiting from another monthly production record in July. Casas Bahia surged 36.4%, bringing its two-session gain to 125% after confirming the anticipation of effects from its judicial reorganization filing.

Financial stocks declined, with TD Bank and BMO down 0.9% and National Bank losing 1.5%. Higher yields also weighed on gold prices, pressuring miners. Agnico Eagle fell 4.4%, Barrick and WPM declined 3.6% and Franco-Nevada shed 2.7%. Tech stocks extended losses, tracking weakness on Wall Street amid soaring AI-related corporate debt issuance. US Stocks Fall Amid Tighter Financial Conditions — US stock indices closed lower on Tuesday on growing concerns that tighter financial conditions could weigh on economic growth. The S&P 500 fell 0.7%, the Dow lost 419 points and the Nasdaq 100 dropped 1.3%. US-Iran strikes in the Persian Gulf prolonged the suspension of energy exports from the region and heightened global inflation risks, reinforcing expectations that major central banks could resume rate hikes this year. Meanwhile, soaring in AI-related corporate debt issuance added to credit supply and pushed yields higher. Tech stocks led losses, with Micron down 2.6%, AMD falling 2.4%, Oracle and Palo Alto Networks each losing 5.2%, and Dell dropping 6.8%. Nvidia fell 1.5% despite a $35 billion computing deal between Lambda and Anthropic. Asset managers also declined, with Blackstone down 4.6% and KKR falling 3.1%.

### 2026-09-02 02:53:50  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【ANTHROPIC releases Fable 5.1 — coding/science gains; 75% cut to re‑call token price; customer cloud hosting option】ANTHROPIC released Fable 5.1, saying the model improves performance on programming and scientific tasks while lowering enterprise operating costs. The model is positioned for long, complex software tasks — including full-application development and code review — and expands scientific capabilities such as experimental design, result simulation and reading complex charts and tables. Enterprise token pricing remains unchanged from Fable 5, but Anthropic cut pricing for re-invoking already-processed context by 75% — a usage segment that can exceed half of tokens in long-text or complex workflows — which Anthropic says should materially lower overall inference costs. Fable 5.1 also upgrades its safety classifier to better detect risky instructions and reduce false positives, targeting safer use in cybersecurity and bio-related applications. Anthropic plans to allow enterprise customers to store strongest-model data on their own cloud infrastructure rather than Anthropic servers, while continuing to apply safety checks.

【US Congress passes stopgap funding bill to avert government shutdown】US Congress passed a stopgap appropriations bill to avert an Oct. 1 government shutdown, funding the government through Dec. 11. The House approved the measure 370-48 after the Senate had already cleared it; the bill has been sent to President Trump and has White House support. Lawmakers have passed none of the 12 annual appropriations for the new fiscal year, requiring this continuing resolution. The measure postpones disputes over Pentagon budget increases and Republican proposals to cut social programs until after the midterm elections. House Republicans also plan to delay consideration of a separate $95bn Iran emergency funding bill, and a proposal to add $350bn to the Pentagon’s base budget has been pushed to the autumn.

【德國指認俄涉萊比錫機場襲擊 關閉俄駐波恩總領館 】德國政府1日稱，俄羅斯應對8月上旬發生在德東部萊比錫機場的無人機襲擊未遂事件負責，並宣佈關閉俄羅斯駐波恩總領事館。德國內政部長多布林特當天在新聞釋出會上說，此次襲擊所涉爆炸物和引爆技術與此前俄羅斯在其他“混合行動”和對烏戰事中使用的相似。襲擊顯示出專業特徵，而具體實施者為“低層級特工”。德國外交部長瓦德富爾在同一場合說，德方已召見俄羅斯駐德國大使，並決定於9月18日關閉俄羅斯駐波恩總領事館，同時終止與位於柏林的文化機構“俄羅斯之家”的相關合同。此外，德國將加強對俄公民的入境檢查，並加大對俄“影子艦隊”的施壓力度。（新華社）

### 2026-09-02 01:09:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Rises to Over 1-Month High — Brent futures rose to $94 per barrel on Tuesday, the highest in over one month, as more strikes in the Middle East prolonged the period of suspended exports from the region. The US launched a new wave of attacks against Iran after both countries exchanged strikes earlier in the week, heightening the conflict even though the US had signaled it would stick to only economic measures against Tehran. Tanker flows were set to drop sharply after reports that GCC nations enabled the export of barrels had allowed for some respite in crude oil and refined goods prices. A greater rebound to crude prices were prevented by major economies tapping on inventory to tame higher import turnover. The US SPR plunged to less than 290 million barrels, the lowest since 1982. On top of that, China significantly cut imports of crude oil and refinery runs in the country were cut by only 1.6 million barrels per day, indicating the cutback in crude consumption despite steady demand for refined product.

Crude Oil Surges Toward $90 — Crude oil futures rose to $89.5 per barrel on Tuesday, the highest in over one month, as more strikes in the Middle East prolonged the period of suspended exports from the region. The US launched a new wave of attacks against Iran after both countries exchanged strikes earlier in the week, heightening the conflict even though the US had signaled it would stick to only economic measures against Tehran. Tanker flows were set to drop sharply after reports that GCC nations enabled the export of barrels had allowed for some respite in crude oil and refined goods prices. A greater rebound to crude prices were prevented by major economies tapping on inventory to tame higher import turnover. The US SPR plunged to less than 290 million barrels, the lowest since 1982. On top of that, China significantly cut imports of crude oil and refinery runs in the country were cut by only 1.6 million barrels per day, indicating the cutback in crude consumption despite steady demand for refined product.

### 2026-09-02 01:09:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Gasoline Extends Gains — US gasoline futures fluctuated strongly around $3.11 per gallon on Tuesday, astraders assessed refining capacity and its implications for fuel-pump prices. EIA data showed the average US regular gasoline price edged down to $4.07 per gallon in the week ending August 31st, despite continued tightness in refined supply, while gasoline inventories for the same week fell 2.536 million barrels. Goldman Sachs has warned of tightness in global refining, with the crack spread near multi-year highs, as Russian refining declined to near multi-year lows and fresh strikes from the U.S. on Iranian targets around the Strait of Hormuz kept transit in the waterway cautious. Strong demand is also supporting prices, with heavy driving expected over Labor Day weekend. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump and Energy Secretary Wright are set to meet US oil refiners on Tuesday to discuss near-term measures regarding refining capacity, which is already operating near full capacity.

Peru Annual Inflation Accelerates Further — The annual inflation rate in Peru's capital, Lima, accelerated to 4.44% in August 2026, marking the fastest pace in nearly three years, up from 4.07% in July. Price pressures have strengthened since March amid a domestic gas crisis, higher global fuel prices, and adverse weather affecting crop yields and fishing. Inflation accelerated for food, rising to 3.9% from 3.5%, and restaurants and hotels (4.3% vs 4.2%), while transport costs surged further by 14.8% after a 14.3% rise. Price growth in housing and utilities also added upward pressure, rising sharply to 3.1% from 1.3%. From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.07%, easing from a 0.29% rise in the prior month.

### 2026-09-02 00:02:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Colombia Manufacturing PMI Rises to 54.3 — Colombia’s Davivienda Manufacturing PMI rose to 54.3 in August 2026 from 52.7 in July, signaling the strongest improvement in factory activity since October 2025. New orders grew at their fastest pace in more than two-and-a-half years, while production growth accelerated to its strongest since February. Finished goods inventories declined for a sixth consecutive month as firms used stocks to meet demand. Manufacturers increased input purchases and inventories, with purchasing activity reaching its strongest level since data collection began in April 2011. Delivery delays eased despite some earthquake-related disruptions. Input cost inflation fell to its lowest level this year, while output price inflation picked up slightly. Employment rose for a fifth consecutive month, while backlogs declined for a seventh month. Business confidence surged to its highest level since July 2019, supported by optimism over the new government administration and expectations of stronger demand.

FTSE MIB Closes Sharply Lower — The FTSE MIB fell 1.3% to 51,915 on Tuesday, as markets assessed the impact on escalating tensions in the Middle East across sectors. Heavyweight financials declined as Eurozone inflation accelerated to 3.3% in August, a nearly three-year high, while rising global bond yields and higher oil prices reinforced expectations of an ECB rate hike next week. In Milan, ongoing banking consolidation remained in focus, with the former Economy Minister Tremonti urging caution over “adventures” in finance. Losses were seen in Intesa Sanpaolo (-1.8%), MPS (-1.4%) and UniCredit (-1%). Utilities remained under pressure as European gas prices climbed to three-year highs. Stellantis fell 2.4% after halting production in Turin due to an engine-component shortage amid stronger-than-expected demand for hybrid vehicles, partly driven by higher fuel prices. On the upside, Tenaris (1.9%) and Eni (1.5%) advanced, tracking higher oil prices as the conflict in the Middle East continued to escalate.

【美聯儲理事巴爾：若通脹未緩解 應做好加息準備】美聯儲理事邁克爾·巴爾表示，如果通脹未能回落，美聯儲應做好加息準備。他警告稱，通脹連續逾五年高於目標水平，價格壓力存在固化風險。巴爾表示，如果未來公佈的資料能夠釋放出通脹降溫的訊號，美聯儲有保持耐心的條件。“如果資料趨勢讓我對通脹正朝著2%目標回落建立起一定信心，那麼我認為我們可以花更多時間評估當前的政策立場，”巴爾週二在華盛頓一場活動的講話稿中表示。“但如果通脹看起來未能充分放緩，我認為我們應果斷採取行動加息。”近期，受伊朗戰爭推高油價、新一輪關稅以及人工智慧資料中心建設帶來的需求激增影響，通脹壓力持續，美聯儲官員正討論如何應對。儘管許多官員仍預計無需加息通脹也會回落，但7月政策會議已顯現意見分歧。

US Dollar Extends Rebound — The dollar index rose to above 99.6, extending the rebound from the three-month low of 98.8 from August 21st, and tracking the surge in yields across the curve inflationary risks aligned with the outlook of a more hawkish Federal Reserve. The US and Iran reignited attacks against each other and further dimmed the small prospects that energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz could improve in the near term. The rise in energy prices added to inflationary pressures shortly after a series of FOMC members, including Chairman Warsh, warned that inflation could warrant a rise in interest rates. The Chairman also noted that the labor market has been under full employment recently, aligned with annual revisions to nonfarm payrolls that were well below recent downgrades. In the meantime, evidence of higher inflation in the Eurozone added to the consensus of an ECB rate hike, limiting the rebound for the DXY.

【Senegal, IMF reach staff-level agreement on $2.2bn lending program】Senegal and the IMF have reached a staff-level agreement on a three-year, $2.2bn program to resume IMF lending after the fund froze financing two years ago following discovery of previously undisclosed loans worth billions of dollars. The agreement requires IMF executive board approval, the IMF said on Tuesday. Senegal said it will use the revised G20 common framework for debt restructuring and intends to seek debt treatment to restore debt sustainability. The IMF said: "The IMF-supported program's main reforms aim to restore the sustainability of public finances while protecting vulnerable households."

### 2026-09-01 23:33:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Oil Extends Gains for 2nd Session — Crude oil rose to around $88 a barrel on Tuesday, following a 2.8% gain on Monday, as renewed hostilities between the US and Iran heightened concerns over prolonged disruptions to energy shipments through the Strait of Hormuz. US forces struck an island in the strategic waterway, prompting Iran to retaliate with attacks on US-linked targets in the UAE and Jordan, marking the first direct exchange of fire in about a month. Two supertankers were reportedly hit by unidentified projectiles while transiting the chokepoint, highlighting the risks facing vessels in the region. Despite the escalating tensions, crude exports continue to move through Hormuz, with some tankers reportedly switching off their transponders. Producers including Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Kuwait and Iraq are still managing to ship barrels. However, the threat of further attacks remains a major concern, particularly after a tanker was reportedly struck by three unidentified projectiles near Oman while exiting the waterway.

### 2026-09-01 23:29:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Hover Near 1-Year High — Cocoa futures traded around $6,600 per tonne, holding close to their highest since September 2025, amid mounting concerns over West African supply. This year’s cocoa crops in top producers Ivory Coast and Ghana have been hit by cloudy weather and limited sunshine, creating conditions favorable for black pod disease and hurting bean quality. The outlook for the upcoming crop is also uncertain due to excessive wetness, disease and limited access to chemicals, with risks potentially compounded by an unusually strong El Niño. Production forecasts for major growers have been lowered, with Ivory Coast’s output expected to fall by about 20% to 1.75 million tonnes and Ghana’s to decline 13% to 650,000 tonnes next season. Elsewhere, farmers in Cameroon are struggling with shortages of chemicals to control pests and diseases, while floods have disrupted cocoa farming in Nigeria.

【歐洲央行管委Simkus：9月加息一次恐怕還不夠】歐洲央行管委Gediminas Simkus表示，即使歐洲央行下週加息，也不足以讓通脹回到目標水平。這位立陶宛央行官員在週二刊發的Econostream Media採訪中表示，“目前所有可獲得的資料都讓我認為，我們很可能會加息，而且9月這次加息恐怕還不夠。”Simkus指出，天然氣期貨價格上漲、農產品價格走高，以及歐洲經濟表現強於預期，都表明消費者價格前景正在進一步上行。Simkus表示，“這應該會促使通脹預測發生變化，從而需要利率路徑略微上移，以確保中期通脹維持在2%。”他表示，“總體而言，通脹環境已經增強，因此支援加息的理由相當明確。”

### 2026-09-01 23:16:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Mexico Factory Activity Weakens — The S&P Global Mexico Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.8 in August 2026 from 51.3 in the prior month, signaling a renewed deterioration in operating conditions. A reduction in factory output weighed most, marking the steepest contraction in eight months as firms reported subdued market conditions, material shortages and shipping delays linked to the conflict in the Middle East. Demand also weakened, as new order growth slowed from recent peaks, reflecting a fall in new export orders at the quickest pace in 2026 so far. As a response, manufacturers trimmed their purchasing activity, marking the first reduction in three months. Stocks of purchases also fell, while fineshed goods inventories rose slitghly. In turn, cost pressures continued to moderate, as the input cost inflation slipped to a five-month low, although still elevated amid US tariff and Middle East uncertainty. Looking ahead, sentiment turned pessimistic, with firms citing concerns over general market uncertainty.

### 2026-09-01 23:12:06  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【道明證券：美財政部加碼長債回購料使10年期期限溢價下降約5個基點】道明證券表示，美國財政部計劃將10年至30年期國債回購規模“至少提高一倍”，如果每次操作的最高回購規模為40億美元，可能令10年期國債期限溢價下降約5個基點。道明證券利率策略師Molly Brooks在一份研究報告中表示：“如果財政部將每次回購操作的最高規模提高至40億美元，每季度可進一步減少180億美元的長端國債供給。我們估計，這將使期限溢價下降約5個基點”。Brooks稱，目前期限溢價已下降約13個基點，“這表明部分市場參與者可能預期回購規模會更大，或者長期國債拍賣規模可能下降”。Brooks表示，縮減長端國債拍賣規模將更符合財政部“定期且可預測”的發債策略，並稱，如果偏離這一可預測模式，反而可能推高期限溢價，並抵消減少長端供給帶來的好處。

### 2026-09-01 23:04:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Wheat Rises Toward 3-1/2-Year High — Wheat rose to around $7.65 per bushel, close to its highest level since February 2023, as renewed attacks in the Black Sea region heightened concerns over grain supplies. Turkey said it had prepared a plan to facilitate the safe movement of grain through the Black Sea and was in contact with both Russia and Ukraine. However, Moscow rejected a proposed moratorium on attacks in the area, arguing that it would not contribute to a peaceful resolution. Ukrainian officials said Russian strikes overnight damaged port facilities and a border crossing with Romania in the Odesa region, while further attacks were reported near Russia’s Baltic port of Ust-Luga. The renewed hostilities have increased uncertainty around exports from both countries, with previous attacks severely disrupting shipments through Black Sea and Sea of Azov ports. Both countries are major exporters, together accounting for more than a quarter of global wheat shipments.

### 2026-09-01 23:04:31  #央行 #國際

【美國7月職位空缺小幅增加 反映勞動力需求穩定】美國7月職位空缺小幅增加，裁員人數下降，顯示企業用工需求仍在較低水平上保持穩定。美國勞工統計局週二公佈的資料顯示，7月職位空缺從6月下修後的718萬個升至727萬個。接受調查的經濟學家的預期中值為731萬個。報告顯示，美國勞動力市場仍延續過去幾年較為典型的“低招聘、低裁員”格局。面對地緣政治不確定性和持續通脹，企業對擴大員工規模保持謹慎，但同時也不願大規模裁員。職位空缺增加主要由製造業、州和地方政府（不包括教育領域），以及醫療保健和社會援助行業推動。其中，製造業職位空缺升至2023年12月以來最高水平，而休閒和酒店業職位空缺降至2021年以來最低。與此同時，裁員人數降至今年1月以來最低水平。製造業解僱人數則降至五年多來的最低。衡量員工每月主動離職比例的所謂離職率小幅降至1.9%。報告還顯示，每名失業者大約對應1.1個職位空缺。該比率在2022年高點時曾達到2比1，美聯儲官員經常用這一指標衡量勞動力供需平衡狀況。

【SpaceX重組資料中心領導團隊】近期，SpaceX對旗下資料中心建設團隊進行了高層人事調整，多名負責人被替換，接任者主要來自公司火箭與衛星網際網路業務。知情人士透露，此舉源於田納西州和密西西比州現有資料中心設施在土木工程與執行可靠性方面持續暴露問題。早期建設階段，SpaceX的AI部門（SpaceXAI）為追求速度，部分設施在數月內缺乏備用冷卻和電力系統，增加了宕機風險。人事變動方面，前獵鷹火箭和龍飛船生產副總裁Wesley Salandro、星艦基地（Starbase）發射場產品支援運營總監Logan McConnell已加入AI基礎設施團隊。此外，SpaceX還從Neuralink引入一名工程師協助土木工程，並持續招聘相關崗位。與此同時，資料中心團隊多名核心成員近期離職：負責物理基礎設施的Jake Palmer於7月底離開（其於2025年9月加入），另有Zach Wells、Pablo Mendoza等；此前，負責場地建設的Brent Mayo已跳槽至OpenAI，從特斯拉Dojo超級計算機專案加入的Liz Balke也轉投Anthropic。

### 2026-09-01 22:42:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Dallas Fed Services Index #Importance 1

Dallas Fed Business Activity Slows — The general business activity index for the Dallas Fed’s service sector fell to 4.2 in August 2026 from 6.6 in the prior month. Despite the modest decline in optimism, the index remained in positive territory, indicating continued improvement in broader business conditions, while uncertainty was broadly unchanged. The revenues index declined to 6.6 from 9.5, while the company outlook index fell sharply to 3.0 from 10.4. Input price pressures intensified, with the index rising to 35.6 from 33.5, while selling-price growth eased to 8.9 from 14.2. Capital spending also weakened to 6.0 from 11.5. Labor-market conditions softened, with the employment index falling to 0.8 from 2.6, although wages and benefits edged higher. Looking ahead, firms remained optimistic, with the future general business activity index rising to 18.6 from 16.8.

### 2026-09-01 22:38:09  #公司 #國際

【瑞銀在瑞士資本新規博弈中暫獲支援】瑞士議員委員會近日投票支援一項折中方案，允許瑞銀（UBS）以發行債券的方式滿足新資本要求的一半，另一半則需透過增發股權來完成。這意味著瑞銀可大幅降低籌資成本，被市場視為階段性勝利。不過，該方案仍需瑞士議會兩院最終表決，尚未塵埃落定。2023年瑞銀緊急收購瑞信後，瑞士政府著手修訂針對系統重要性銀行的資本規則，擬要求瑞銀持有遠高於當前的資本，以防範其美、英大型子公司一旦崩盤拖累整個集團。瑞銀則堅決反對加碼，認為瑞信倒閉源於商業模式失敗和治理薄弱，而非資本不足。瑞銀計劃採用的AT1債券（應急可轉債）本為銀行提供額外損失緩衝，但此類工具爭議不斷——瑞士當局在救助瑞信時直接將170億美元AT1債券減記至零，持有人蒙受鉅額損失，多起訴訟至今未了結。

TSX Falls on Rising Global Yields — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 0.5% to trade below 36,500 on Tuesday amid rising global bond yields. Renewed hostilities in the Middle East lifted oil prices, prompting markets to reassess expectations for interest rate hikes by central banks. The BoC is expected to leave rates unchanged on Wednesday, while Canada’s 10-year yield reached a near two-week high. Financial stocks traded mostly lower, while higher yields also weighed on gold prices, pressuring miners. Agnico Eagle fell nearly 3%, while Barrick, WPM and Franco-Nevada lost about 2%. Tech stocks extended losses, with Shopify down more than 3.5%. In contrast, energy stocks gained, with Canadian Natural, Suncor and Cenovus adding around 2% each.

Ibovespa Rises on Enegy Gains and Selic Cut Expectations — The Ibovespa rose nearly 1% to trade close to 179,000 on Tuesday, supported by energy shares and renewed expectations of continued Selic easing. Petrobras gained more than 1.5% as renewed hostilities in the Middle East lifted oil prices, while PRIO added over 1%. Higher energy costs and expectations of a Fed hike pushed global bond yields higher, pressuring financials and broader markets. Still, signs of a gradual moderation in economic activity raised expectations for further Selic cuts, supporting the index. Brazil’s GDP grew 0.5% in the second quarter of 2026, but a decline in household spending reflected weakness for consumers. Although the BCB has begun an easing cycle, the Selic rate remains elevated at 14%. Utilities were mostly higher, while retailers gained about 1%.

### 2026-09-01 22:12:12  #央行 #市場 #國際

【全球債市“壓艙石”鬆動 日本10年期收益率重返3%意味著什麼？】全球債券市場迎來又一個歷史性時刻。9月1日盤中，10年期日債收益率一度升至3%，為1996年10月以來首次突破這一關鍵心理關口。這一變動不僅意味著日本債券市場執行邏輯或迎來根本性轉變，更引發了一場席捲歐美等地的全球債市拋售潮。業內人士表示，這場劇烈拋售恰逢中東局勢再度升級，布倫特原油期貨價格重返每桶91美元上方，通脹擔憂捲土重來。與此同時，美國政府債務突破40萬億美元、日本政府計劃擴大財政支出、科技巨頭為AI基礎設施大規模融資，多重因素疊加，正在深刻重塑全球債券市場的定價邏輯。道富環球投資管理東京高階固定收益策略師提供了一個關鍵洞察，“10年期日債收益率觸及3%無疑是一個里程碑，但我更傾向於將其視為正常化程序，而非危機訊號。市場正在為更高通脹環境、更高中性利率以及日央行進一步加息的預期進行重新定價。”他進一步指出，真正值得關注的不是大規模資金突然迴流，而是日本逐漸“不再成為海外債券的邊際買家”。（新華財經）

### 2026-09-01 22:11:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Construction Spending #Importance 1

US Construction Spending Falls 0.5% in July — Construction spending in the US fell 0.5% month-over-month to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of $2,158 billion in July 2026, following a upwardly revised flat reading in June and missing market expectations of a flat reading. Residential construction spending fell 1.3%, while nonresidential spending edged up 0.1%. Within the nonresidential sector, the largest declines were recorded in conservation and development (-1.4%), manufacturing (-1%) and public safety (-1%). These were partly offset by gains in office construction (2.9%). Meanwhile, both private (-0.5%), dragged by a 3.2% decline in new single family residential construction spending, and public (-0.2%) construction spending fell over the month. In the first seven months of 2026, total construction spending fell 3.5% from the same period a year earlier to $1.245 trillion.

### 2026-09-01 22:08:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

US Manufacturing Activity Revised Higher: S&P Global — The S&P Global US Manufacturing PMI was at 53.9 for a third consecutive month in August of 2026, revised higher from the flash estimate of 53.2, and aligned with the initial market expectations. Both output and new order growth eased in the period. Surveyees noted higher cost levels, with input inflation remaining well above the historical levels due to tariff uncertainties and the war in the Middle East, forcing companies to raise their charges. These developments drove firms to cite muted client appetite, resulting in the slowdown for new business. Still, higher production environments had manufacturers raise their purchasing activity for the eighth month running. Looking ahead, companies remained optimistic about their business, underpinning another period of employment growth.

### 2026-09-01 22:08:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Job Quits #Importance 1

US Job Quits Fall in July — The number of workers voluntarily leaving their jobs in the United States decreased by 157,000 to 3.06 million in July 2026, from a revised 3.21 million in the prior month. Quits fell primarily in accommodation and food services (-86,000); professional and business services (-77,000) and other services (-49,000). By region, quits declined in the West (-265,000) and the Northeast (-22,000) but increase in the Midwest (+114,000) and the South (+16,000). The quits rate edged down to 1.9% from 2%, remaining near its lowest level since 2020 and signaling continued reluctance among workers to quit.

### 2026-09-01 22:05:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Business Confidence #Importance 3

US Manufacturing Growth Slows in August — The ISM Manufacturing PMI fell to 54.6 in August 2026 from July’s near four-year high of 55.6, below market expectations of 55.2. The reading nevertheless marked the eighth consecutive month of expansion in manufacturing activity, although growth moderated as new orders slowed sharply to 53.7 from 56.7. Production growth was broadly stable at 58.3, compared with 58.5 in July, while employment growth eased to 51.2 from 52.8, pointing to a softer pace of job creation in the sector. Meanwhile, manufacturers continued to highlight rising costs, supply-chain disruptions and heightened uncertainty, with tariffs and the Middle East conflict adding further pressure. The Prices Index remained elevated at 71.1, while the Supplier Deliveries Index rose to 59.3, signaling continued supply-chain delays. The Backlog of Orders Index fell sharply to 51.8, while the Imports Index declined to 52.5, suggesting weaker order pipelines and import activity.

US Job Openings Below Forecasts — Job openings in the US increased by 89,000 to 7.271 million in July 2026 from a downwardly revised 7.182 million in June, below market expectations of 7.30 million. The number of job openings increased in durable goods manufacturing (+76,000), health care and social assistance (+54,000), wholesale trade (+50,000), and construction (+28,000). On the other hand, job openings fell in transportation, warehousing, and utilities (-67,000) and professional and business services (-65,000). Regionally, openings rose in the Midwest (84,000) and the West (135,000), but fell in the Northeast (-28,000), and the South (-102,000). Over the month, hires and total separations both changed little at 5.1 million. Within separations, quits (3.1 million) and layoffs and discharges (1.7 million) were little changed.

### 2026-09-01 21:55:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Canada Manufacturing PMI Holds Expansion — The S&P Global Canada Manufacturing PMI edged lower to 53.0 in August 2026 from 53.5 in July, remaining above the 50.0 no-change threshold for a fifth consecutive month. Production and new orders both increased for the fifth straight month, with output growth accelerating to its fastest pace since April. Employment also rose, with job creation reaching its strongest level since October 2024. Manufacturers increased purchasing activity and marginally raised input inventories, partly reflecting precautionary buying amid concerns over prices and product availability. Input cost inflation remained elevated despite easing, while output price inflation also slowed from July. Meanwhile, business confidence strengthened in August, reaching its highest level since late 2024.

### 2026-09-01 21:35:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Drop Sharply — US stocks fell on Tuesday on growing concerns that tight financial conditions will hamper economic growth. The S&P 500 and the Dow fell up to 0.7%, while the Nasdaq 100 fell nearly 2%. Strikes between the US and Iran in the Persian Gulf prolonged the suspension of energy exports from the region and added to pro-inflationary risks on the global economy, consolidating the likelihood that major central banks are due to restart their hiking cycles this year. On top of that, soaring AI corporate debt issuance added to credit supply and lifted yields further. Tech stocks led the losses on tightened credit conditions, with Marvell, Intel, Oracle, and Tesla dropping around 3%. Meanwhile, Nvidia dropped 2% despite te $35 billion computing deal between Lambda, where the chip giant has a large stake, and Anthropic. Meanwhile, asset managers fell further with Blackstone and KKR dropping 4% each.

### 2026-09-01 21:22:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Arabica Coffee Futures at Over 2-Month Low — Arabica coffee futures eased toward $3 per pound, reaching their lowest since late June, pressured by expectations of higher output from top producer Brazil. The 2026/27 coffee harvest was 97% complete by August 26, according to Safras & Mercado, with Arabica at 96% and the country on track for a potentially record crop..Rabobank echoed the favorable supply outlook, saying it still expects a “very comfortable” global coffee balance in 2026/27, with a surplus of 8.9 million bags. However, it noted that delays in Brazil and earthquake-related problems in Colombia are keeping stocks tight, with significant replenishment unlikely before November.. Arabica coffee stocks monitored by ICE continued to fall, reaching 223,976 bags by August 31, the lowest level in 27 years. Meanwhile, next year’s harvest remained in focus, with the outlook clouded by erratic weather and the risk of a strong El Niño potentially delaying critical rainfall during the September-October flowering period.

### 2026-09-01 21:15:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Israel #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Bank of Israel Cuts Key Rate to 3.25% — The Bank of Israel lowered its policy rate by 25 bps to 3.25% at its September 2026 meeting, missing markets expectations of a hold, delivering a third straight rate cut and bringing the policy rate to its lowest level since 2022. Policymakers noted that inflation has moderated, and the rate under review is below the mid-point of the target range of 1%-3%. Meanwhile, economic activity grew at a rapid pace in the first half of the year, while, excluding production abroad, activity appears more moderate. However, policymakers also noted that uncertainty remained high against the backdrop of geopolitical tensions, although the risk premium remained near similar levels. Labor market conditions remain tight, although with a slightly increase in broad unemployment. Looking ahead, inflation is expected to remain near the mid-point of the target range.

### 2026-09-01 21:13:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Brazil Manufacturing PMI Weakens — The S&P Global Brazil Manufacturing PMI fell to 46.3 in August 2026 from 47.5 in July, signaling a second consecutive monthly deterioration in sector conditions and the steepest contraction in 40 months. Output declined for the fourth consecutive month and at the fastest pace in more than three years. New business fell for the seventeenth straight month, with the contraction accelerating from July. New export orders declined for the fourth consecutive month and at the fastest pace in more than three-and-a-half years. Weaker demand weighed on staffing and capacity, with employment falling for a second month and at the fastest pace since October 2025. Backlogs also declined further. Inventory and purchasing data pointed to cautious operating conditions, while price pressures eased for a fourth consecutive month. Despite the weak activity data, manufacturers remained optimistic about the year-ahead outlook.

### 2026-09-01 20:56:58  #市場

【瑞銀最新研判：A股風險因素邊際改善，市場重回多板塊輪動】“這輪調整更多體現為市場在前期上漲後的風險釋放，而非中期趨勢的根本性逆轉。隨著融資餘額回落，市場去槓桿程序逐步接近尾聲。與此同時，維護資本市場平穩執行的積極因素正加速累積，市場修復基礎持續夯實，這些都有助於下半年‘慢牛’行情的延續。”瑞銀全球研究部亞太區總監連沛堃9月1日在瑞銀證券A股研討會上稱。瑞銀全球金融市場部中國主管房東明也稱，在風險逐步釋放、政策預期改善和盈利趨勢向好的共同作用下，A股市場正在進入修復和重估的新階段。相較上半年A股市場行情高度集中於科技板塊，瑞銀中國股票策略研究主管王宗豪認為，AI相關板塊仍值得持續關注，但下半年投資者也會關注非AI板塊，多板塊輪動機會增多。瑞銀證券中國股票策略分析師孟磊也稱，看好成長風格，但對大盤、小盤的相對風格配置，建議投資者進行更為均衡的配置。（第一財經）

### 2026-09-01 20:56:12  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【Dreame Technology halts car mass-production plan; auto unit folded into research institute, MOVA partly retained】On Sept. 1 Dreame Technology said it has abandoned plans for mass production of vehicles and will fold auto-related operations into the company’s industry research institute, shifting the unit to focus on generic technology exploration and tech commercialization. Dreame said it will retain only technical reserves for the car project. MOVA, launched in 2024 as a global high-end AI smart‑life brand covering robot vacuums, floor washers, lawn mowers, personal-care appliances and planned consumer electronics, will not be fully shut. Dreame said home-cleaning lines with high overlap between MOVA and the Dreame core brand—particularly robot vacuums—will be consolidated to eliminate duplicate R&D and supply‑chain investment. Outdoor/yard robot assets that align with the group’s strategic businesses will be retained and moved into a unified outdoor/yard operations track.

Baltic Dry Index Snaps 7-Day Winning Streak — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, fell about 0.9% to 3,157 points on Tuesday, halting a streak of seven straight sessions of advances. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also snapped a seven-day winning run, dropping by 2.1% to 5,221 points. On the other hand, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, rose 1.9% to 2,360 points, marking the seventh session of gains; and the supramax index went up 0.2% to 1,650 points.

### 2026-09-01 20:55:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Chile IMACEC Declines More Than Expected — Chile’s IMACEC economic activity index fell 1.5% year-on-year in July 2026 after rising a downwardly revised 2% in June, missing forecasts for a 0.4% increase and marking the sharpest decline since March 2026. Goods production fell 3.2%, driven by a 9.3% decline in mining, particularly copper, due to lower ore grades, maintenance work and adverse weather. Manufacturing output fell 3.1%, also affected by weather, with lower production of fuels and chemical products. Services declined 0.7%, reflecting weaker personal, business, transportation, restaurant and hotel services. Trade activity rose 0.7%, driven by retail sales, partly offset by lower wholesale activity. The non-mining IMACEC fell 0.3%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, economic activity decreased 1.7%.

⑨GoPro美股盤前一度漲超106%，現漲71%，報1.5美元。此前Youtube一名超4000萬粉絲的網紅成為該公司最大股東，並向媒體大肆吹噓GoPro產品。

【*ST清越：收到證監會行政處罰事先告知書】*ST清越公告稱，公司收到中國證監會《行政處罰事先告知書》。經查，公司涉嫌欺詐發行、資訊披露違法違規：證券發行檔案編造重大虛假內容，2020年至2022年上半年分別虛增利潤總額337.40萬元、764.53萬元、2358.16萬元，佔各當期利潤總額的5.56%、15.58%、131.74%；2022年度虛增利潤總額4711.56萬元，佔當期披露利潤總額的108.53%，2023年上半年虛增利潤總額4753.60萬元，佔當期披露利潤總額絕對值的145.10%，且未及時披露補繳4441.99萬元出口退稅款事項。證監會擬對公司責令改正、給予警告，並處1.72億元罰款；對高裕弟等4名相關責任人分別處以400萬元至1050萬元不等罰款，並分別採取3至8年證券市場禁入措施。公司可能觸及重大違法強制退市情形，最終處罰結果以證監會正式決定書為準。

【歐洲央行管委雷恩：我們承受不起歐洲出現購買力危機】歐洲央行一位高層政策官員警告，歐洲央行必須為中東地區一場曠日持久的消耗戰做好準備，該局勢或將推高歐元區通脹。此番表態發表於市場預期歐洲央行將於下週加息的背景之下。 雷恩在接受採訪時談及受衝突推升的能源價格，以及霍爾木茲海峽航運通道近乎封鎖的現狀，他表示：“面對這些通脹壓力，我們絕不能自滿。我們承受不起歐洲出現購買力危機。” 雷恩並未公開明確支援9月加息，但他表示，市場對下週會議的定價“可以理解”。對於後續利率決策，他態度更為審慎，指出廣泛存在的地緣政治不確定性，利率政策必須“逐次會議評估”。雷恩警示稱：“我們必須做好衝突長期化的準備。特朗普政府與伊朗革命衛隊雙方訴求完全對立，中東或將陷入一場消耗戰。” 他同時指出，歐元區今年經濟增長展現出“驚人韌性”，面對高能源價格與貿易緊張，經濟體的表現好於市場此前擔憂。

【Foreign investment in UK energy falls to 12-year low】Foreign investment in the UK energy sector fell to a 12-year low, with foreign-backed projects halving to 27 last year, Ernst & Young data showed. Oil and gas projects fell to three from 16 a year earlier. The Labour government has paused new North Sea drilling and retained a windfall tax on oil and gas producers; the sector is braced for Chancellor John Healey’s first budget, where tax rates may rise further, and uncertainty remains over approval of new drilling licences. An Ernst & Young survey of foreign investors cited high UK energy costs as the biggest deterrent, along with concerns about political stability and high labour costs; respondents identified an uncertain economic outlook—covering growth, interest rates and public debt—as the principal risk to UK attractiveness over the next three years.

### 2026-09-01 20:21:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Current Account #Importance 1

India’s Current Account Deficit Widens on Higher Trade Gap — India’s current account deficit widened to $4.2 billion, or 0.5% of GDP, in Q1 of fiscal 2026-27, from $3.4 billion, or 0.4% of GDP, a year earlier. The increase was driven by a wider goods trade deficit, which rose to $86.1 billion from $68.9 billion as imports jumped 20% to $218.0 billion amid higher energy prices, while exports grew 17.1% to $132.0 billion. The impact was partly offset by an improvement in the primary income balance, with the deficit narrowing to $10.5 billion from $13.3 billion. Meanwhile, the services surplus rose to $51.6 billion from $47.9 billion, while the secondary income surplus increased to $40.8 billion from $30.9 billion.

【美眾議院尋求對消費者金融保護局進行重大改革】美國眾議院金融服務委員會主席、阿肯色州共和黨眾議員弗倫奇·希爾（French Hill），以及領導該委員會金融機構小組委員會的肯塔基州共和黨眾議員安迪·巴爾（Andy Barr），週二提出了一項法案，尋求對消費者金融保護局（CFPB）進行重大改革。CFPB長期以來一直是共和黨的主要改革目標。該法案將透過國會為CFPB提供資金，使這一機構更加直接地受國會議員控制。目前，消費者金融保護局的資金來自美聯儲轉撥，而這種資金安排一直存在爭議。該法案還將針對CFPB的規則制定程式建立新的監管護欄。其中包括允許白宮管理和預算辦公室（OMB）主任羅素·沃特（Russ Vought）在重大規則正式生效後的最長八年內，對這些規則進行追溯性審查。

【“貨幣貶值交易”再度升溫 金礦股創數十年來最佳8月表現】過去一個月，金礦股近乎垂直上漲，吸引了此前因華盛頓政策制定者釋放不同訊號而頻繁遭遇市場波動的投資者重新入場。地緣政治動盪和財政不確定性推動金礦股在八月創下至少自1994年以來的最佳表現，漲幅超過現貨黃金的三倍多。儘管美聯儲主席沃什承諾抑制通脹後，市場在8月底經歷劇烈波動，但紐約證交所Arca金礦股指數仍上漲33%，從3月創下的歷史高點回落39%後強勁反彈。VanEck黃金礦業ETF(GDX)錄得2月以來最高的月度資金流入。而金價本身在8月份僅上漲了10%。美國財政部試圖壓低長期借貸成本，促使投資者重新湧向黃金及相關資產。由於成本相對固定，礦業公司能夠放大金價上漲帶來的收益，因此被視為押注貴金屬進一步上漲的槓桿工具。

### 2026-09-01 20:15:21  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Magnificent 7 #Star Stocks #Apple #AAPL.O #Impact mixed #GOOGL.O #Impact bullish

【From partners to courtroom: Apple-OpenAI clash underscores intensifying AI competition】Apple sued OpenAI in federal court in July 2026, accusing the company of stealing trade secrets and using Apple intellectual property to build its own consumer hardware. Apple alleges OpenAI executives and former Apple employees orchestrated a large-scale effort to obtain confidential information about unreleased Apple products and that OpenAI disclosed Apple secrets when engaging Apple partners. On Aug. 3 Apple sought a preliminary injunction to bar OpenAI from using the disputed information during litigation; OpenAI responded the same week with a blog post saying Apple is wrong and on Aug. 5 asked a federal judge to dismiss the case as meritless. In filings on Aug. 19 Apple pushed the case into discovery; on Aug. 31 OpenAI countered in court papers, saying Apple failed to safeguard its own information and is likely to lose. A hearing on the matter is scheduled for Oct. 1, 2026. Context: Apple and OpenAI began partnering in June 2024 when Apple integrated ChatGPT into iPhone, iPad and Mac, with OpenAI CEO Sam Altman attending Apple’s event. Signs of strain emerged in May 2025 after OpenAI acquired Jony Ive’s startup io for $6.4 billion and signaled an AI hardware push. In Jan. 2026 Apple shifted strategy toward software-led AI integration, partnering with Google to run Apple Intelligence features on Google’s Gemini model and cloud infrastructure.

【"Currency-debasement trade" revives; gold miners post best August in decades】Gold miners surged over the past month, drawing back investors hit by earlier policy-driven volatility. Geopolitical tensions and fiscal uncertainty helped the sector record its strongest August since at least 1994: the NYSE Arca Gold Miners Index rose 33% in August, more than three times the roughly 10% gain in spot gold, rebounding after a 39% drop from March highs. Markets swung late August after Fed chair KEVIN WARSH pledged to curb inf, but VanEck Gold Miners ETF (GDX) posted its largest monthly inflows since February. U.S. Treasury moves to lower long-term borrowing costs pushed flows into gold and related assets; with largely fixed operating costs, miners provide leveraged exposure to higher gold prices.

Brazil GDP Grows More than Expected — The Brazilian GDP expanded by 0.5% in the second quarter of 2026, extending the 1.1% expansion from the first quarter and ahead of market expectations of a 0.4% growth rate. Output remained supported by the elevated level of government spending that has boosted household consumption in recent quarters, in addition to measures to ease access to bank loans. Hence, government expenditure expanded by 0.4% and gross fixed capital formation surged 1.2%. Still, high inflation and borrowing costs due to the widening public deficits reflected some deterioration in household consumption, which contracted by 0.4%. Net foreign demand dented the GDP sharply, with exports falling 0.8% while imports surged 1.8%. From the previous year, the Brazilian GDP expanded by 2%, inching up from the 1.8% in the first quarter.

### 2026-09-01 19:55:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Pull Back Sharply — Futures tracking US equity indices were firmly lower on Tuesday on growing concerns that tight financial conditions will hamper economic growth. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Dow were 0.5% lower, while those for the Nasdaq 100 fell more than 1%. Strikes between the US and Iran in the Persian Gulf prolonged the suspension of energy exports from the region and added to pro-inflationary risks on the global economy, consolidating the likelihood that major central banks are due to restart their hiking cycles this year. On top of that, soaring AI corporate debt issuance added to credit supply and lifted yields further. Tech stocks led the losses on tightened credit conditions, with Marvell, Intel, and Sandisk dropping more than 2.5%. Also, Amazon, Tesla, and Nvidia fell over 1%.

【人民銀行上海總部：用好創業擔保貸款等相關政策，更好支援婦女創業企業發展】9月1日，據人民銀行上海總部官微訊息，近日，人民銀行上海總部、上海金融監管局、市婦聯聯合召開金融活水賦能“她發展”——落實金融支援婦女就業發展政策專題座談會。會議介紹了《中國人民銀行  金融監管總局  全國婦聯關於進一步加強金融支援婦女就業發展的實施意見》（以下簡稱《意見》）的出臺背景，並就重點內容進行深入解讀。《意見》是國內金融領域面向女性群體的專項扶持政策，從加大金融資源投入、最佳化教育培訓金融服務、健全保險保障機制、強化服務能力建設、加強政策和組織保障五大維度推出16條舉措。《意見》鼓勵金融機構圍繞“巾幗電商”、手工製作、家政服務等居家靈活就業新模式制定個性化金融服務方案；針對婦女就業創業特點，創新專屬信貸產品和服務模式，提高信貸供給適配性；充分運用金融科技手段，提高獲客能力、風險評估能力和貸款審批效率。

### 2026-09-01 19:51:25  #市場 #國際

【俄羅斯原油三個月來首次在印度獲得溢價】受美伊局勢升級以及烏克蘭襲擊俄羅斯能源設施影響，全球原油供應風險加劇，俄羅斯原油自 5 月末以來首次在印度市場出現溢價交易。資料顯示，俄羅斯主力出口品級烏拉爾原油透過油輪從西部港口運往印度西海岸，週四與週五較實物交易國際基準布倫特現貨原油出現每桶 0.4 美元的溢價。資料顯示，按絕對值計算，週五印度西海岸的烏拉爾原油價格升至每桶 90.10 美元。該價格包含航運成本，而俄羅斯西部港口原油均價為每桶 63.425 美元。目前尚不清楚這一交割價差，即出口價與到岸價之間的差額，是否會歸俄羅斯所有。該價格仍高於俄羅斯本年度預算設定的每桶 59 美元基準油價。俄羅斯財政約五分之一的收入來自油氣行業，因此能夠從中東衝突中獲益。

【美國加州加速收緊資料中心監管 要求上報能源消耗、設立專項電價】面對資料中心大規模建設在全美各地引發的反對聲浪，美國加州議員正加緊出臺約束該行業的法規，在州議會會期的最後一週一口氣透過七項法案，對行業實施更為嚴格的監管。 就在特朗普總統週一在社交平臺呼籲各地接納資料中心建設，否則就會變得“落後且貧窮”之後數小時，上述法案的最後一批條文獲得表決透過。這也是加州針對公眾對資料中心日益高漲的批評，迄今為止力度最大的政策回應。 這批法案要求資料中心向政府機構上報能源與水資源消耗資料，專案動工前必須開展環境影響評估，同時執行特殊電價機制，避免資料中心將成本轉嫁至其他電力使用者。 特朗普在社交平臺發文力挺資料中心擴張之後，加州州長加文・紐森接受記者採訪時表示：“如果我們要發展資料中心，就要把這件事做規範。” 這位加州州長稱：“總統的想法是放任行業野蠻生長。但完全無視公眾輿論，對行業本身並無益處。”

### 2026-09-01 19:44:47  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【歐元區8月通脹率升至3.3%】歐盟統計局9月1日公佈的初步資料顯示，2026年8月歐元區通脹率按年率計算為3.3%，較7月的2.9%明顯上升。資料顯示，8月歐元區能源價格同比上漲14.3%，漲幅較7月的10.3%明顯擴大，是推高整體通脹率的主要因素；服務價格同比上漲3.0%，低於7月的3.3%；非能源工業產品價格同比上漲1.2%，高於7月的0.9%。食品、酒類和菸草價格同比上漲1.2%，漲幅與7月持平。剔除能源、食品、酒類和菸草價格後，8月歐元區核心通脹率為2.4%，較7月的2.5%略有回落。從國別看，8月歐元區主要經濟體通脹率普遍回升。其中，德國通脹率從7月的2.8%升至2.9%，法國從2.4%升至2.7%，義大利從2.9%升至3.2%，西班牙從3.9%升至4.5%。歐洲中央銀行理事會成員、芬蘭央行行長奧利·雷恩日前警告，面對當前通脹壓力，歐洲央行“絕不能掉以輕心”。（新華社）

### 2026-09-01 19:25:48  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Russia cuts 2026 oil output forecast to lowest in 17 years】A government draft forecast shows Russia has cut its 2026 oil output forecast to a 17-year low, citing the Russia-Ukraine war. The draft also trims fuel export forecasts for 2026–27 and will be finalised at end-September for use in the budget. Compared with projections published in May, 2026–29 oil output forecasts have been reduced by 16–20 mln tonnes. In the base case, Russia’s crude output will fall 17.2 mln tonnes this year to 494.2 mln tonnes (9.88 mln bpd), the lowest since 2009. Output is expected to recover to 500.0 mln tonnes next year but remain 16 mln tonnes below the prior forecast; 2028–29 production is projected to rise further yet stay below 2025 levels.

【部分鋼廠接受焦炭四輪提漲 呂梁低硫主焦煤最高漲至2800】國內煉焦煤市場延續偏強執行態勢。供應端，長治市沁源縣今日新增復產1座煤礦，另有1座煤礦預計明日復產，主產地部分煤礦雖有序推進復產，但實際增量釋放有限，區域流通貨源整體偏緊，對煉焦煤價格形成較強支撐。今日多地主流大礦價格普遍上漲，其中山西主流大礦上漲170-350元/噸，平頂山主流大礦上調280-350元/噸，山東主流大礦上調120-160元/噸，河北主流大礦上調100-220元/噸。線上競拍市場同樣漲勢強勁，臨汾安澤低硫主焦精煤成交均價2547元/噸漲142元/噸；呂梁中陽低硫主焦煤成交均價2798元/噸漲236元/噸，孝義高硫主焦煤成交均價2348元/噸漲206元/噸；晉中靈石高硫主焦精煤成交價2464元/噸漲136元/噸。需求端，受煉焦煤價格持續衝高影響，焦化企業虧損局面未得到實質性修復。今日焦炭開啟第四輪提漲100-110元/噸，3日起執行，部分鋼廠已接漲。當前焦鋼企業原料庫存處於低位，去庫表現顯著，仍存在一定的補庫需求。綜合來看，短期煉焦煤供給偏緊的核心矛盾仍未緩解，市場呈現易漲難跌格局，預計後續維持震盪偏強執行。 (我的鋼鐵網)

### 2026-09-01 19:12:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Finishes Almost Muted — India’s BSE Sensex closed virtually unchanged at 76,944 on Tuesday, as traders weighed ongoing foreign fund outflows, geopolitical and global inflation concerns against robust domestic economic data. Oil prices continued to advance amid renewed fighting between the US and Iran, raising concerns over inflation and interest rates. Meanwhile, India's manufacturing sector expansion cooled slightly in August, while the economy beat estimates to grow 7.8% in the quarter through June, ahead of the RBI's projection of 7%. Selling pressure in banking, autos and healthcare stocks was partly offset by gains in techs and selected heavyweights. Maruti Suzuki shares fell more than 4%, making its the worst performer, after the automaker reported August sales, with exports declining over 7% year-on-year.SBIN, IndiGo, Bajaj Finserv, M&M and Axis Bank also faced pressure, falling between 1.8%-1.5%. On the flip side, shares of ITC (4%), HCL Tech (3.2%), Infosys (2.4%) and Bharti Airtel (2.3%) stood out.

【OneRail 聯合英偉達推出面向零售商的人工智慧平臺，助力其更快做出配送決策】物流企業 OneRail 即將推出一款平臺，該平臺採用英偉達人工智慧軟體，助力零售商大規模快速選定最高效的配送方案。這款名為 OmniStar 的全新平臺，依託 OneRail 的專有資料，讓零售商借助人工智慧評估全部配送選項，為每一筆獨立訂單篩選最優配送方案。這家末端配送企業表示，新平臺能夠幫助中小型企業實現規模化配送、提升利潤率，以此同亞馬遜、沃爾瑪等全球零售巨頭展開競爭。隨著電商行業發展，零售商必須應對激增的市場需求，投入搭建靈活的供應鏈以提升運營效率。但這類人工處理流程，往往在零售商與物流服務商之間處於割裂狀態。OneRail 執行長比爾・卡塔尼亞表示：“倘若無法做到極速決策，就會損失利潤，末端履約的成本十分高昂。”OneRail 介紹，以往為包裹選定最優配送路線可能需要 20 分鐘，藉助人工智慧，該平臺僅需 2 分 30 秒即可完成。卡塔尼亞稱，節省下來的時間，能夠幫助零售商搭建規模更大、響應更快、精度更高的供應鏈。OneRail 人工智慧業務負責人戴維・戴施勒表示：“這正是人工智慧發揮作用的地方。它可以極速完成這類決策，而實現該能力，就需要英偉達的軟硬體，真正保障平臺大規模落地執行。”

### 2026-09-01 19:03:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Slides as Higher Yields Strengthen Fed Rate-Hike Bets — Silver prices fell more than 2% to below $65 an ounce on Tuesday, their lowest level since August 19, as elevated US Treasury yields pressured the non-yielding metal, while investors await key US labour-market data due later this week. US Treasury yields climbed to their highest level since January 2025 as heightened Middle East tensions fueled inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for a near-term Fed rate hike. Last week, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh said at the Jackson Hole symposium that the central bank would “have work to do” if policymakers were not confident inflation was returning to its 2% target. Markets are currently pricing a 66% probability of a rate hike later this month, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Attention now turns to the ADP employment report on Wednesday and nonfarm payrolls on Friday for further signals on the Fed’s policy path.

### 2026-09-01 18:59:25  #MKT News

【UK military expects Healey to pledge 3% of GDP defence spending by 2030】The UK military expects Chancellor Healey to commit to raising defence spending to 3% of GDP by 2030, despite criticism he has backtracked on earlier promises. Officials said the Treasury informed military leaders weeks ago it could not include the pledge in the Oct. 28 budget and that a final decision will be taken at the multi-year spending review in 2027. After discussions with Healey, sources expect a target date of 2030 to be set. Defence spending currently equals 2.6% of GDP; the Office for Budget Responsibility estimates a rise to 3% would cost about £15 billion a year, equivalent to roughly a two percentage-point increase in the basic income tax rate. How the additional spending would be funded remains unclear.

【現房新政塵埃落定！中海150億元上海拿地，市場盼地方細則打通銜接壁壘】8月31日，上海土地市場完成第八批次集中住宅用地出讓。這是8月28日住房城鄉建設部、自然資源部、金融監管總局聯合印發《關於完善商品住房銷售制度的通知》後，核心城市舉行的首場土地出讓活動。本輪共出讓兩宗地塊，分別為普陀區“真如之心”商住辦組合地塊和松江區泗涇低密純宅地，兩宗地塊全部成交，合計收金156.01億元。百億“地王”固然是市場焦點，但業界更為關注的是，新出讓地塊是否會按照新政要求實行現房銷售制度。對此，中海地產上海公司相關人士回應稱，目前尚不確定，需等待上海方面的實施細則出臺。這一態度也反映了當前多數房企的普遍心態，在觀望中等待政策進一步落地。但也有房企人士認為，雖然地方細則尚未落地，但政策已出，大方向已定。（中國房地產報）

French Bond Yields Hit Highest Since 2008 — France’s 10-year OAT yield climbed above 4.2%, its highest level since November 2008, as investors digested fresh inflation data that strengthened expectations for another ECB rate hike this month, with the Iran war continuing to put upward pressure on prices. Eurozone inflation accelerated to 3.3% in August, its highest since September 2023 and well above the ECB’s 2% target, driven by surging energy prices. Markets now price the ECB’s deposit rate at around 2.70% by December, implying an 80% probability of a second hike following an expected September move. ECB policymakers Olli Rehn and Martin Kocher warned that prolonged conflict and rising inflation risks could require further tightening. Meanwhile, hawkish comments from Fed Chair Kevin Warsh pushed markets to price in a 66% probability of a September Fed hike.

### 2026-09-01 18:35:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Rises to Over 1-Month High — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield moved higher to around 8.85%, the highest in over a month, tracking an extended global selloff driven by growing inflation concerns linked to the prolonged Middle East conflict. Rising oil prices amid escalating hostilities between the US and Iran reinforced inflation expectations, which increased the prospect of interest rate hikes by major central banks. In South Africa, inflationary pressures are building again, with further fuel price adjustments due to take effect this month. Higher fuel costs are expected to raise transport and other prices, potentially feeding through to the broader economy and complicating the inflation outlook. South Africa’s annual inflation fell to 4.3% in July from 5% in June, marking its first decline in five months, mainly on the back of slowing prices of fuels and food. The SARB is set for another difficult decision this month, weighing inflationary pressures against the need to support the economy.

UK Mortgage Borrowing Falls Sharply in July — Net mortgage borrowing by UK individuals fell to £4.3 billion in July, down from £7.7 billion in June and below the previous six-month average of £5.3 billion. The annual growth rate of net mortgage lending was unchanged at 3.6%. Gross secured lending also eased to £25.9 billion from £26.9 billion, slightly below its six-month average of £26.4 billion. Repayments edged up to £21.3 billion from £21.2 billion, remaining above the six-month average of £20.8 billion. The effective interest rate on newly drawn mortgages rose to 4.45% in July from 4.35% in June, while the rate on the outstanding mortgage stock increased slightly to 3.97% from 3.96%.

### 2026-09-01 18:24:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

North Macedonia Industrial Output Growth Eases in July — North Macedonia’s industrial production increased 1.3% year-on-year in July 2026, slowing sharply from 6.5% in the previous month. Output growth eased in manufacturing (3.4% vs 7.9% in June), particularly for food products, textiles, wearing apparel, and other transport equipment, while declines deepened in mining and quarrying (-5.9% vs -3.8%) and electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (-19.5% vs -8.2%). By industrial grouping, production fell for capital goods (-2.9% vs 8.6%) and durable consumer goods (-9.9% vs 8.6%), while the decline in energy deepened (-22.2% vs -11.1%). Output of intermediate goods excluding energy also slowed (9.2% vs 11.7%). In contrast, output of non-durable consumer goods rose faster (3.1% vs 2.0%). In the January-July period, industrial output increased 0.9% from the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-09-01 18:23:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Total Vehicle Sales #Importance 1

Spain Car Sales Reach Highest August Level Since 2019 — Spain’s new passenger car registrations rose 11.8% year-on-year to 68,544 units in August 2026, marking the strongest August since 2019. Sales returned to growth despite the holiday period, supported by strong demand for electrified vehicles, which accounted for more than a quarter of the market. Rental registrations posted the strongest growth, rising 32% to 2,268 units, followed by business sales, which increased 13.1% to 27,167 units. Private registrations rose 9.9% to 39,109 units. Year to date, 818,201 new passenger cars have been registered, up 6.3% from a year earlier. Electrified vehicle registrations, including BEVs and PHEVs, jumped 27% in August to 18,967 units, giving them a 27.7% market share. So far this year, 185,340 electrified vehicles have been sold, up 34.1% year-on-year and accounting for 22.6% of total registrations.

### 2026-09-01 18:19:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Nigeria #Composite PMI #Importance 1

Nigeria Private Sector Activity Gains Momentum — The Stanbic IBTC Bank Nigeria PMI rose to 54.3 in August 2026 from 52.5 in July, signalling a solid improvement in Nigeria’s private-sector conditions. The latest data pointed to the seventh consecutive month of growth and the most pronounced since March 2025. New orders strengthened sharply, growing at their fastest pace since the start of 2024, on improving customer demand and new product launches. Companies responded by accelerating business activity, with the rate of output growth significantly stronger than in July. Improved material availability was also cited as a factor supporting growth. Employment rose for a 15th consecutive month, but job creation remained modest. Backlogs fell for the first time in seven months, while purchasing activity accelerated. Purchase cost inflation rose slightly in August, driven by higher fuel and transport costs, with output price inflation also accelerating. Firms remained confident about future growth, despite a slight dip in sentiment.

### 2026-09-01 18:15:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

10-Year AGB Yield Soars to 15-Year High — The yield on the 10-year Australian Government Bond surged to over 5.17% in September, the highest since 2011, and tracking the rise in global bond yields on expectations of higher interest rates by major central banks. The return of strikes between the US and Iran added to pessimism on energy supply from the Middle East and rekindled pro-inflationary risks in major economies. This magnified the impact of an overshot domestic inflation print in July, when headline inflation rose to 3.6%, ahead of expectations of 3.3%. Markets increased exposure to hedges against a incoming rate hike by the RBA after minutes from the central bank's last meeting reflected growing concerns over price growth. Global inflation also raised bets on hikes by the Fed and BoJ this year. On top of that, record corporate debt issuance, including Australian dollar offerings by Alphabet, also lifted yields.

### 2026-09-01 18:13:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #LMI Logistics Managers Index #Importance 2

US Logistics Sector Slows for 2nd Straight Month in August — The US Logistics Managers’ Index fell for a second consecutive month to 66.6 in August 2026, from 68.9 in July, pointing to the slowest growth in the logistics sector in six months. The decline was driven by a slowdown in inventory expansion, while logistics costs continued to rise at a high pace. Inventory Levels eased (-2.2 to 52.8), primarily driven by Upstream. However, expansion in Inventory Costs continue apace (+1.6 to 78.6), which is their second-fastest rate of expansion in 12 months. There were similar dynamics in warehousing and transportation. Warehousing Capacity moved up (+7.2 to 53.5), its fastest rate of expansion all year, while Warehousing Prices remained elevated (-0.5 to 75). Transportation Capacity is still contracting (+11.6 to 40) but Transportation Price expansion is up (+3.1 to 90). Essentially, the August report paints a picture of logistics costs that seem to be rapidly increasing no matter what the underlying situation is.

Brent Tops $92 — Brent rose above $92 a barrel on Tuesday, following a 2.7% gain on Monday, as renewed hostilities between the US and Iran heightened concerns over prolonged disruptions to energy shipments through the Strait of Hormuz. US forces struck an island in the strategic waterway, prompting Iran to retaliate with attacks on US-linked targets in the UAE and Jordan, marking the first direct exchange of fire in about a month. Two supertankers were reportedly hit by unidentified projectiles while transiting the chokepoint, highlighting the risks facing vessels in the region. Despite the escalating tensions, crude exports continue to move through Hormuz, with some tankers reportedly switching off their transponders. Producers including Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Kuwait and Iraq are still managing to ship barrels. The reading showed that factory growth improved for a third straight month, reaching its strongest level since May 2022, driven by a solid rise in new orders, particularly in the intermediate goods sector, as well as stronger export sales. There was a range of factors driving demand, including increased defence spending, data centre build-outs and stockpiling. Also, the mood among businesses improved noticeably and is now the brightest seen since the Middle East conflict began. However, there was also a worsening of supply chain pressures, which in turn contributed to a marked rise in purchasing activity as firms looked to secure supplies of inputs. Average prices paid for purchases meanwhile rose at the slowest rate since the start of the Middle East conflict, but one that was still elevated by historical standards.

### 2026-09-01 16:05:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

FTSE MIB Trades Lower — The FTSE MIB fell to around 52,300 on the first trading day of September, following a subdued performance in the previous session, weighed down by rising oil prices amid escalating tensions in the Middle East. Higher oil prices have further fueled inflation concerns, increasing expectations of additional ECB rate hikes. Financials came under the most pressure amid continued uncertainty surrounding Italy’s banking consolidation wave, with the largest declines seen in UniCredit (-0.6%), Intesa Sanpaolo (-1.3%), Mediobanca (-1.5%), Banca Mediolanum (-1%), Unipol Gruppo (-0.7%), and MPS (-1.2%). On the flipside, oil major Eni gained more than 1%. On the economic data front, Italy’s GDP growth was confirmed at 0.2% quarter-on-quarter in Q2, while the manufacturing PMI slipped back into contraction in August. Markets will await more data later in the day, including the unemployment rate for July and preliminary inflation figures for August.

### 2026-09-01 15:59:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Italy Manufacturing Slips Back Into Contraction — The S&P Global Italy Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.6 in August 2026, down from 51.3 in July and below market expectations of 51.5. The reading, though only marginally below the 50.0 threshold, marked the first deterioration in operating conditions since January, with output, new orders and purchasing stocks all weighing on the index. New orders declined for a second consecutive month and at the fastest pace in nearly a year and a half, while export orders also fell. Weaker production requirements led to the first contraction in manufacturing output in seven months, driven by a decline in intermediate goods production. Purchasing volumes also fell, posting their sharpest decline in almost a year. Supply-chain disruptions remained a key constraint amid material shortages and transport issues, although the deterioration in supplier delivery times eased to a six-month low. Meanwhile, employment continued to rise, helping underpin firms’ expectations for growth over the coming year.

### 2026-09-01 15:46:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Inflation Rate Hits 1½-Year Low — The annual inflation rate in Kazakhstan eased to 9.8% in August 2026 from 10.2% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since February 2025. Food inflation slowed to 9.5% from 10.1% in July, particularly for meat and poultry (12.9% vs 15.8%), sausages and meat products (14.3% vs 15.9%), and oils and fats (9.6% vs 10.6%). Price growth also slowed for non-food products (11.4% vs 11.7%), with softer price increases in clothing (10.2% vs 10.7%) and petrol (12.8% vs 15.3%). Paid services inflation edged down to 8.9% from 9.2%, despite faster price growth for housing and utilities (6.3% vs 5.7%), housing maintenance and servicing (5.6% vs 5.0%), and maintenance, repair and security of residential premises (13.1% vs 12.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.6%, unchanged from July and remaining the lowest reading since March.

### 2026-09-01 15:45:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Swiss Factory Activity Hits 3-Month High — Switzerland’s procure.ch–UBS Manufacturing PMI climbed to 57.1 in August 2026, picking up from a five-month low of 53.2 in the previous month. It marked the highest reading since May, as the renewed acceleration in the sector was driven by stronger performances across several key components, particularly production (up 5.6 points to 59.8), order books (up 4.2 points to 57.5), purchasing volumes (up 12.9 points to 61.0), and employment (up 2.7 points to 50.4). Inventory indicators also improved, with stocks of purchased goods rising 0.7 points to 48.2 and sales stocks increasing 2.4 points to 49.2, though both remained slightly below the neutral 50 mark. Meanwhile, supplier delivery times lengthened, with the corresponding index climbing 4.0 points to 66.9. On the price front, input cost pressures eased, as the purchasing prices index fell 4.9 points to 69.3.

European Stocks Cautious to Kick Off September — European stocks were broadly flat to higher on Tuesday as investors kicked off September by looking past another rise in oil prices amid escalating tensions between the US and Iran. The increase in energy prices is adding to inflationary pressures and strengthening expectations that the ECB will raise interest rates further. On the data front, Eurozone flash inflation figures due later in the session are expected to reinforce expectations for further monetary-policy tightening. Sterling Slides as Oil Surge and Hawkish Fed Lift Dollar — The British pound fell toward $1.35, its weakest level since August 19, as renewed risk aversion driven by higher oil prices weighed on the currency, while hawkish signals from the Fed continued to support the US dollar. Brent crude rose as renewed hostilities in the Middle East heightened concerns over further disruptions to regional energy flows. Meanwhile, markets are pricing around 32 basis points of BoE tightening by year-end, with a November hike seen as almost 70% likely and a second hike by February priced at around 80%. Rate expectations were further reinforced by the latest British Retail Consortium report, which showed UK shop-price inflation accelerating to its highest level in two years. At the same time, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh said inflation has not slowed meaningfully and that the Fed still has “work to do,” prompting markets to price in a 66% probability of a September rate hike.

### 2026-09-01 15:38:25  #MKT News #EURUSD #Impact bearish #DAX

【Eight months to French election, French assets show political-risk stress】With eight months to France’s presidential election, French assets are showing strain. France’s borrowing premium over Germany, a key sovereign-risk gauge, is nearing its highest level since the 2012 euro-area debt crisis. Stress has spread to French equities and corporate bonds, leaving French assets lagging peers. France’s fiscal deficit tops 5% of GDP, borrowing costs are rising and growth is weak, leaving the economy close to recession. Investors’ central question is how a successor would address these entrenched fiscal and growth challenges. In last week’s first presidential debate, far-right frontrunner Marine Le Pen and far-left Jean-Luc Mélenchon proposed contrasting measures including higher spending, a lower retirement age, partial cancellation of government debt and proposals to suspend certain payments to the EU.

### 2026-09-01 15:37:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Taiwan #Stock Market #Importance 1

TAIEX Ends at 2-Month High as Tech Stocks Rally — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, climbed 820 points, or 1.8%, to 46,949, gaining for the fifth straight session and hitting its highest level since July 1, mainly buoyed by gains in electronic technology. Electronic technology rose 2.0%, while technology services jumped 6.4%. TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker, accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, advanced 0.8%. MediaTek surged 9.9% after Nvidia subscribed to USD 3.5 billion of its exchangeable bonds as part of MediaTek's record USD 3.9 billion overseas convertible bond offering. Meanwhile, Delta Electronics and Hon Hai Precision Industry gained 0.8% and 1.6%, respectively, while ASE Technology Holding soared 4.5%. The fresh data also lifted sentiment, as a private survey showed that Taiwan's factory activity grew solidly in August. However, a fall on Wall Street overnight capped gains, as rising oil prices raised concerns over inflation and reinforced expectations of a Fed rate hike.

FTSE 100 Falls on Tuesday — The FTSE 100 fell around 0.4% on Tuesday, underperforming a broadly weaker European market as gains in oil majors Shell and BP were outweighed by declines elsewhere. Brent crude climbed above $90 a barrel, adding to concerns that persistent energy costs could keep inflationary pressures elevated. Domestically, the latest BRC data showed UK shop price inflation accelerating to its highest level in two years, with prices rising 1.5% year-on-year in August and food prices increasing 2.8%. Meanwhile, UK house prices rose 0.2% in August, slightly faster than in July and ahead of expectations, according to Nationwide. Merger and acquisition activity also remained in focus. Telecoms company Gamma Communications received a takeover proposal from UK private-equity firm Epiris, while FTSE 250-listed Bodycote agreed to a £1.65 billion acquisition by US investment firm Veritas Capital after a competitive bidding process, with shareholders offered around 940 pence per share in cash.

### 2026-09-01 15:35:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Czechia Factory Growth Strongest Since 2022 — The Czech Manufacturing PMI rose to 54.1 in August 2026 from 52.2 in July, marking the strongest improvement in operating conditions since April 2022. Growth accelerated in output and new orders, with new business rising at the fastest pace since February 2022, supported by stronger international demand. New export sales also increased at a solid pace, though growth eased from July. Employment resumed growth as firms responded to stronger demand, while backlogs accumulated at the fastest pace since December 2021 amid worsening supplier delays, material shortages and logistics issues. Input cost and selling-price inflation eased but remained elevated. Firms increased purchasing and safety stocks, while finished-goods inventories declined. Business confidence also strengthened to a five-month high, supported by expectations of stronger demand and planned machinery investment.

### 2026-09-01 15:29:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

UK Natural Gas Prices Surge — UK natural gas prices jumped more than 4% to above 172 pence a therm on Tuesday, the highest level since January 2023, as the flare-up in US-Iran tensions heightened concerns over Europe’s gas supply security heading into the heating season. The US and Iran exchanged strikes for the first time in a month over the weekend, threatening to prolong the effective closure of the Strait of Hormuz, through which roughly one-fifth of global LNG trade normally passes. The prospect of sustained disruption has intensified competition with Asian buyers, tightening supplies bound for Europe during the critical gas-storage refill season. EU gas storage facilities were around 64% full, below historical levels for this time of year. Low storage levels and uncertainty over LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf are putting upward pressure on prices, offsetting milder weather forecasts for early September that could limit near-term demand.

### 2026-09-01 15:26:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Spain Manufacturing Sector Contracts in August — The S&P Global Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.5 in August 2026 from 50.2 in July, the lowest since March and missing expectations of 50.1. Both production and new orders declined, reflecting subdued domestic demand and weaker international sales, with tariffs also weighing on exports. Firms responded by drawing down finished-goods and input inventories, while purchasing activity contracted for a ninth month. Employment also fell for the twelfth month in a row, although the decline in outstanding work was partly supported by lower capacity utilisation. Supply conditions remained challenging, as disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict affected shipping routes and the availability of materials. Higher energy and transportation costs pushed input-price inflation significantly higher, prompting firms to raise selling prices as well. Looking ahead, business confidence weakened further, with concerns over elevated energy costs and fragile demand clouding the outlook for production.

### 2026-09-01 15:26:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Hungary Manufacturing Growth Edges Lower in August — Hungary’s Manufacturing PMI edged down to 51.3 in August 2026 from 51.4 in July, remaining above the 50-point threshold that separates expansion from contraction. The reading fell below its long-term monthly average of 52.7 but remained above the average August level of 49.7 recorded over the past three years, indicating continued growth in manufacturing activity despite a slower pace. The moderation reflected weaker new orders, although they remained in expansion territory, while production volumes fell from the previous month but continued to expand. Meanwhile, the purchase price index eased but continued to signal a strong rise in input costs. The employment index edged down but remained in expansion, while both export and import indicators also pointed to continued growth.

### 2026-09-01 15:21:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Austria Jobless Rate Rises to 7.2% — Austria’s unemployment rate rose to 7.2% in August 2026, up 0.2 percentage points from a year earlier. The total number of unemployed persons increased by 10,027 year-on-year to 311,448. Among them, female unemployment rose 6.3% to 151,513, while male and other genders’ unemployment increased 0.7% to 159,935. Dependent employment was estimated at 4,003,000, up 10,000 from August 2025. Including training participants, the number of unemployed and enrolled in training programs reached 375,730, rising 2.3% year-on-year. Female unemployment and training participants increased 5.8%, while the number of unemployed and training participants among men and other genders fell 0.8%. Long-term unemployment also remained elevated, with those unemployed for more than 12 months increasing 22.6% to 55,995.

### 2026-09-01 15:16:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Austria Inflation Rises to 3.2% in August — Austria’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.2% in August 2026, up from 2.8% in the previous month, preliminary estimates showed. Prices increased for energy, which jumped 10.0% year-on-year from 5.7% in July. Moreover, prices for food, tobacco, and alcohol rebounded to 0.2% from a 0.1% decline, although gains were still capped by the reduction in VAT on basic foodstuffs from 10.0% to 4.9%, which took effect in July 2026. In contrast, price growth for manufactured goods moderated to 0.5% from 0.8%, while services continued to exert the strongest upward pressure on inflation despite easing slightly to 4.3% from 4.4% a month earlier. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.6% in August, following a flat reading in the preceding period.

【習近平出席上海合作組織成員國元首理事會第二十六次會議並發表重要講話】當地時間9月1日上午，國家主席習近平在比什凱克出席上海合作組織成員國元首理事會第二十六次會議。

上海合作組織輪值主席國吉爾吉斯斯坦總統扎帕羅夫主持會議，上海合作組織成員國白俄羅斯總統盧卡申科、印度總理莫迪、伊朗總統佩澤希齊揚、哈薩克總統託卡耶夫、巴基斯坦總理夏巴茲、俄羅斯總統普京、塔吉克總統拉赫蒙、烏茲別克總統米爾濟約耶夫以及上海合作組織秘書長葉爾梅克巴耶夫等參加。

習近平同上海合作組織成員國領導人集體合影，隨後出席元首理事會會議並發表題為《推動上海合作組織實現更高質量發展》的重要講話。

習近平指出，今年是上海合作組織成立25週年。25年前，面對冷戰的深刻教訓、“三股勢力”的現實威脅和各國人民對和平與發展的熱切期盼，維護主權、安全、發展利益成為地區國家普遍共識。上海合作組織因此應運而生，並日益顯示出強大生機活力。25年來，上海合作組織走過不平凡歷程。最重大的發展成就是一步步成為世界上幅員最廣、人口最多、發展潛力巨大的新型區域合作組織；最寶貴的精神財富是開創性提出和始終遵循互信、互利、平等、協商、尊重多樣文明、謀求共同發展的“上海精神”；最重要的合作經驗是找到不結盟、不對抗、不針對第三方的正確共處之道，推動地區國家攜手建設共同家園。25年後的今天，世界百年變局加速演進，對話和對抗、共贏和零和、多邊和單邊激烈博弈，戰爭陰霾並未遠去。站在關乎人類前途命運的十字路口，上海合作組織應該勇擔歷史責任，主動引領歷史航向。（新華社）

### 2026-09-01 15:12:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

European Bond Rout Deepens on Rate Hike Bets — European government bonds extended their sell-off in early September, with Germany’s 10-year Bund yield climbing above 3.3% for the first time since May 2011. French yields rose to their highest level since November 2008, while Dutch yields reached 15-year highs and Italian and Spanish yields climbed to near two- and three-year highs, respectively. The renewed pressure on sovereign debt came as rising oil prices and increasingly hawkish signals from major central banks reinforced expectations for higher interest rates. Markets are now pricing the ECB’s deposit rate at around 2.7% by December, implying roughly an 80% probability of a second rate hike after an expected move as early as September. Meanwhile, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh warned that inflation has not slowed meaningfully and that the Fed still has “work to do,” prompting markets to price in a 66% probability of a September rate hike.

### 2026-09-01 15:10:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Turkey Manufacturing Activity Falls at Softer Pace — The Istanbul Chamber of Industry Türkiye Manufacturing PMI ticked up to 48.1 in August 2026 from 47.7 in July, reaching its highest level in three months. The reading signalled a modest easing in the deterioration of business conditions, although the Middle East conflict continued to weigh on the sector, keeping demand muted amid widespread uncertainty. Both total new orders and new export business declined, albeit at slower rates than in July. In turn, manufacturers scaled back production for the third consecutive month, although the pace of contraction remained modest. Firms also reduced employment and purchasing activity, with both falling at solid rates that were sharper than in July. Stocks of both purchases and finished goods also dropped further. Input cost inflation accelerated to a three-month high, driven by higher fuel and oil costs as well as rising raw material prices. This prompted firms to raise their selling prices. Moreover, suppliers' delivery times lengthened.

【商務部合作司負責人解讀《汽車行業境外競爭行為與合規建設指引》】一是突出公共服務屬性。《汽車指引》作為海外綜合服務體系建設的一項具體成果，是服務汽車行業企業出海的公共服務產品，有助於企業及時規範境外競爭行為、加強合規建設，提高國際化經營水平和影響力。

二是突出實踐應用導向。《汽車指引》制定過程中，充分結合了我國企業境外生產經營現狀，參考了行業領軍企業合規實踐經驗，為汽車行業企業出海提供可操作指引。鼓勵企業因地制宜，結合有關法律法規、反壟斷政策、市場原則及商業慣例要求，在落實中不斷調整完善自身工作。

三是突出合作共贏理念。《汽車指引》強調依法合規、公平競爭、互利共贏的基本原則，有助於引導汽車行業企業積極在境外履行社會責任，更好融入東道國經濟社會發展；有助於擴大汽車行業高水平開放合作，促進全球汽車產業鏈供應鏈深度融合、共同發展。

下一步，商務部將會同有關部門，持續推動完善海外綜合服務體系，更好引導汽車行業合理有序跨境佈局。

Poland Manufacturing Downturn Deepens in August — Poland's S&P Global Manufacturing PMI fell to 48.3 in August 2026 from 49.0 in July, missing forecasts for a rise to 49.6 and signaling a steeper deterioration in factory activity. Output and new orders declined at faster rates, with new work falling for the 17th straight month amid weaker demand and increased competition from mainland China. New export orders also fell for a ninth consecutive month, albeit modestly. Employment declined after rising in July, while purchasing activity fell for the sixth time this year. Supplier delivery times lengthened at their fastest pace since June 2022 amid production delays and transport issues. Meanwhile, input cost inflation accelerated for the first time in four months, while output price inflation eased to a six-month low. Despite weaker activity, business confidence improved, with the Future Output Index recording its biggest monthly rise of 2026 so far, supported by expectations of new contracts and improved market conditions.
